Implementation:
```
def _transfer_top_bottom(a, frac, amount, rng, sum_target=1.0):
   b=a.copy()
   m=len(b)
   q=int(max(1, frac*m))
   top_idx=np.argpartition(b, -q)[-q:] if q<m? 
   bot_idx=np.argpartition(b, q-1)[:q] # if q>0
   # Compute total excess above target? For exact flattening, maybe set top and bottom to new values preserving sum.
```
Better: For top and bottom sets, compute mean of all? We can move a fraction of difference: For each top i, bottom j sorted? We can do global: b[top_i] -= excess*amount; b[bot_j] += excess*amount; clip nonnegative/renormalize.
Simpler: Use log-space direction d = -np.sign(a - median)/max? Or use gradient of c0: d = -(a - mean)/ (a+eps). Multiplicative `b = a * exp(s*d)`; normalize. This directly flattens. We can try s values.
2. `active_pairwise(a, c, k, count, strength)`: For active lag k:
   - valid i range as above.
   - sample random indices i in range; j=k-i.

def _normalize(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,dtype=float),0.0)

`_conv_full(a)`: For n, use direct if n <= 2048? Actually direct O(n^2); for n=3809, direct in C may be faster than FFT? ```
def _conv_full(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n < 512:
      c=np.convolve(a,a)
   else:
      N=1
      m=2*n-1
      while N < m: N <<=1
      fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
      c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:m]
   c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0? no
   c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
   return c
```
Need if a length 1, N loop while N<1? N=1? rfft length1 okay. Use `while N < max(1, 2*n-1)`. For n=1, N=1.

def _internal_score(a):

def _active_lags(c, count=8, random_count=4, rng=None):

Gradient direction function:
```
def _grad_lag(a,k): as above
def _direction_from_lags(a, c, lags, rng):
   g=np.zeros_like(a)
   weights=[]
   M=float(np.max(c))
   for k in lags:
       w=(float(c[k]) / (M+1e-30))**4 maybe; or max(0, c[k]-M+0.1*M)
       g += w * _grad_lag(a,k)
   if norm==0 return None
   # Normalize direction by max abs
   g -= g.mean()
   return -g / max(abs(g).max(), eps)  # sign because want decrease sum w c_k
```
Then multiplicative update `b = a * np.exp(step * d)` where d is normalized. Since g is gradient of weighted c, moving -g decreases. Pairwise transfer function:
```
def _pairwise_transfer(a, k, count, strength, rng, do_diagonal=False):
   b=a.copy()
   n=len(b)
   if k==0:
      js=0; je=n
      for _ in range(count):
         i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
         if i==j: continue
         ai=b[i]; aj=b[j]
         if ai<=aj: i,j=j,i; ai,aj=aj,ai
         delta = strength * (ai-aj)
         if delta <=0: continue
         delta=min(delta, ai*0.5, strength*ai) # can't take more than half to keep nonneg
         b[i]-=delta; b[j]+=delta
   else:
      js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)+1
      if js>=je: return a
      # if even, half diagonal at k/2; ignore or handle
      for _ in range(count):
         j=rng.integers(js, je)
         i=k-j
         if i==j:
             # move from diagonal index to another valid paired index? maybe reduce a[i] to a[i2]
             i2 = int(rng.integers(js,je))
             if i2==i: continue
             ai=b[i]; aj=b[i2]
             if ai<=aj: continue
             delta=strength*(ai-aj)
             delta=min(delta, ai*0.5)
             b[i]-=delta; b[i2]+=delta
         else:
            pair keys; to avoid duplicate, maybe if i>j? doesn't matter.
            ai=b[i]; aj=b[j]
            if ai<=aj: continue
            delta=strength*(ai-aj)
            delta=min(delta, ai*0.5)
            b[i]-=delta; b[j]+=delta
   return _normalize(b)
```
But for k>0, moving from larger pair element to smaller reduces product a_i a_j (good) but may create imbalance in other lags. Strength small.

Global flattening: `pairwise_transfer` with k=0, count ~max(100,n//20). Use strengths [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2].

Smoothing via exponential moving average:
```
def _smooth_linear(a, alpha=0.5, iters=1):
   b=a.copy()
   n=len(b)
   for _ in range(iters):
      b0=b.copy()
      # neighbor average with zero padding
      b[1:-1] = (1-alpha)*b0[1:-1] + alpha*(b0[:-2]+b0[2:])*0.5
      b[0] = (1-alpha)*b0[0] + alpha*b0[1] if n>1
      b[-1] = (1-alpha)*b0[-1] + alpha*b0[-2]
   return _normalize(b)
```
This reduces in-place with zero boundary; okay.

Fourier low-pass:
```
def _smooth_fft(a, beta=0.2):
   n=len(a); N=1; while N<2*n: N<<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   k=np.arange(len(fa)).astype(float)
   # zero freq no decay
   fa[1:] *= np.exp(-beta*k[1:]*(1.0/n))  # smoothing scale n
   b=np.fft.irfft(fa,N)[:n]
   return _normalize(np.maximum(b,0))
```
Boundary okay.

Random block:
```
def _block_perturb(a, rng, width_frac, sigma):
   b=a.copy(); w=max(2,int(len(b)*width_frac)); s=rng.integers(0,len(b)-w+1); b[s:s+w]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,size=w)); return _normalize(b)
```

Let's implement a log-space Adam smooth optimizer:
```
def _log_adam_run(a, n, deadline, tag, ...):
   w = np.full(n, -np.log(n))  # but use a to get log
   a = normalize(a)
   w = np.log(a + floor)
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   tau = max_c/4 or 1/(4*n)
   lr=0.1; betas...
   best...
   for it until deadline:
      c = conv_full(a); mc=max(c)
      o=2n mc
      update best
      # If c all? p=softmax(c/tau)
      p = softmax(c, tau)  # tau in c units
      g = 2*correlation_fft(p,a)  # derivative of sum p c; (not scaled by 1/tau)
      gn = max(abs(g))
      if gn>0: g/=gn
      gw = a*(g - np.dot(a,g))
      # Adam on w
      m = b1*m + (1-b1)*gw
      v = b2*v + (1-b2)*gw*gw
      mh = m/(1-b1**t); vh=v/(1-b2**t)
      update = lr*mh/(sqrt(vh)+eps)
      w_new = w + update
      w_new = w_new - max(w_new)  # stable
      a_new = exp(w_new); a_new /= sum
      # backtracking: if obj(a_new) > obj(a)*(1+1e-8), halve lr? or only accept if improves
      o_new = obj(a_new)
      if o_new < o - 1e-12: accept, maybe lr=min(lr*1.05, 0.5)
      else: reject, lr=lr*0.5
      tau = max(tau*0.999, max_c*1e-4)
      if stall... random kick
```

Convolution c via FFT: Need N >= 2n-1. We can compute `fa = rfft(a,N)`, `c=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2n-1]`. This c should match q? For sum=1.

Now log-gradient `gw = a*(g - np.dot(a,g))`. If g normalized by max, `gw` scale maybe 1/n. Adam handles.

Acceptance: We can run Adam updates; if objective improves, keep; else backtrack/reject. Instead of standard Adam with rejected updates remain? In Adam, m,v update even if reject? Could, but maybe waste. Simpler: use stochastic gradient with adaptive step: compute update direction, try lr, if not improve shrink lr and stay. Accept update if improve; else try half. Use momentum m,v updated only on accepted? Might be okay.

Pseudo:
```
lr=0.1; b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
m=np.zeros_like(w); v=...
t=0; stall=0; last_best_obj=obj
for it in range(10_000_000):
  if time>deadline: break
  c=conv(a); mc=max(c); o=2n mc
  if o < best_obj - 1e-10: best...
  if o >= best_obj and it>0: stall++ else stall=0
  tau = max(tau*0.999, mc*0.005)
  p=softmax(c,tau)
  g=grad(p,a); norm_g=max(abs(g)); if norm_g<1e-300 break
  z = g/norm_g
  gw = a*(z - np.dot(a,z))
  t+=1; m=b1*m+(1-b1)*gw; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*gw*gw
  mh=m/(1-b1**t); vh=v/(1-b2**t)
  step = lr * mh/(sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
  # Clip step to avoid huge: step=clip(step, -10,10)
  candidate = exp(w + step); normalize
  # Backtrack: try slight shrink if candidate worse
  accepted=False
  for f in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]:
      cand = exp(w + f*step); normalize
      o_c = internal(cand)
      if o_c < o - 1e-12 or it%100==0: # accept occasional equal
          candidate=cand; w=...; a=cand; lr*=(1.05 if f==1 else 1.0); accepted=True; break
      # else continue with smaller step
  if not accepted:
      lr = max(lr*0.5, 1e-6)
      # if very frequent no accept, random kick
      if stall > 50: random kick (a *= exp(noise small), reset m/v?); stall=0
  # occasionally sym? no.
  # if stall large, spectral_update
```
But computing internal for each backtrack requires conv FFT; 1-4 per iter, okay. Could compute candidate from w using `np.exp`; stable: `u = w + step; u -= max(u); e=np.exp(u); a=e/e.sum()`. Use `w` normalized? We can keep w raw; after update subtract max for stability, but m,v correspond to w; subtracting constant doesn't affect a or gw? It changes w but Adam state okay if we subtract constant from w after storing? We can `w_new = w + step; w_new -= w_new.max()` and assign. The constant shift has zero effect on grad in simplex? Actually softmax invariant. m,v for w unchanged if we use step; okay.

However `gw` formula assumes a loss with a=exp(w)/sum; if we shift w, same. Good.

Temperature: Since c scale changes, `tau` in c units. If `mc` decreases, tau should decrease. Use `tau = max(tau * 0.998, mc * 1e-3, 1e-15/n)`. Start `tau = max(mc * 0.05, 1/(100*n))`. For target mc ~0.75/n ~2e-4, tau min ~2e-7. p becomes very peaked at active max. Good.

But if tau min relative to mc, p at max only, smooth approximates hard. That's final.

Hard refinement: After Adam smooth, do exact active set with similar log-Adam but p one-hot at max lags. Or simply continue Adam with tau small. The p at exact max if tau very small. To handle ties, if tau < tiny, p may underflow to one lag. We can instead construct p uniform over all lags with c >= mc*(1-1e-12). Use manual weights for active set. Let's implement a hard gradient function:
```
def _active_soft_weight(c, mc, frac=1e-5):
   w = (c - (mc*(1-frac))) / max(mc,eps)
   w = np.maximum(w,0)
   w += 1e-6
   return w/w.sum()
```
This p over active top. Use in gradient. Then continue log-Adam.

Second-order improvement: The active-set gradient direction may not be best. We can use "pairwise transfer" to directly reduce active lag. Since we have exact c and active lags. For a log-space update, direction for active lag k in log variables is proportional to `- (grad_k - a·grad_k) / a?` Actually log gradient already. But we can design targeted pairwise:
For active lag k >0, c_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}. To reduce c_k while preserving sum, moving mass between paired indices can reduce product. In log-space, a transfer from i to j corresponds to direction e_i - e_j (ratio change). The derivative of c_k = g_k_i delta_i + g_k_j delta_j. Since g_k_i=2a_{k-i}, g_k_j=2a_{k-j}. If i+j=k, g_i=2a_j, g_j=2a_i. Transfer from larger a_i to smaller a_j: delta_i=-ε a_i, delta_j=+ε a_j. Change c_k ≈ -2ε a_i a_j + 2ε a_i a_j =0 first order? Wait product a_i a_{k-i} changes second order? Let a_i'=(1-ε)a_i, a_j'=(1+ε)a_j if i,j pair, product = (1-ε)(1+ε)a_i a_j ≈ a_i a_j - ε^2..., small. So first-order no change; need move to unpairs. Better transfer from an element with high paired product to a low one not in pair? The gradient direction does that.

Pairwise transfer for diagonal c0? c0 is a0^2 only? Wait earlier corrected: convolution c_k has diagonal squares at lag 2i, not sum squares. For k=0, c0=a0^2. So to reduce c0, reduce a0 specifically. The central peak maybe sum of pairs. Gradient direction better.

Thus use gradient.

We can also do coordinate descent: For each coordinate i, a_i appears in many c_k: c_i (with a0), c_{2i} (a_i^2), etc. Hard.

Spectral update is more global.

Let's refine spectral update. It can be used as a candidate generator in the log-Adam loop when stalled. It may jump to a different basin. Implementation top-level `_spectral_candidate(a, alpha)`:
- n=len(a). Choose N = 1 << ((4*n).bit_length()) to avoid wrap and have tail. But for 4n=15236, N=16384. Okay.
- We want to modify autocorrelation C to reduce peaks while preserving frequency nonnegative.
Detailed:
```
def _spectral_candidate(a, alpha=0.5, n_windows=...):
   n=len(a); N=1
   while N < 4*n: N <<=1
   ap = np.zeros(N); ap[:n]=a
   A = np.fft.fft(ap)
   C = np.fft.ifft(np.abs(A)**2).real
   C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
   total = C.sum()
   if total < 1e-300: return a
   C = C/total
   L=2*n-1
   valid = np.arange(L)  # 0..2n-2
   # sym tail indices N-valid[-1:]? no
   T = 1.0/L
   # For valid lags, blend towards T but don't force below current? Use cap:
   Cnew = C.copy()
   # reduce lags above T by factor, raise lags below maybe not (would increase max elsewhere)
   high = Cnew[valid] > T
   Cnew[valid[high]] = T + alpha*(Cnew[valid[high]] - T)  # move down
   low = Cnew[valid] < T
   # maybe set low to T? To maintain sum, tail will decrease? If we raise low, total may increase >1; normalize later. But target flat requires raising low. Let's blend low up:
   Cnew[valid[low]] = T - alpha*(T - Cnew[valid[low]])  # move up
   # Need ensure sum=1; normalize Cnew to total 1? If normalize, targets shift. Better adjust tail:
   s_valid = Cnew[valid].sum()
   tail_indices = np.concatenate([np.arange(L, N-2*n?)...]) ???
```

Now, can we solve the problem by spectral methods more directly? We need nonnegative time domain and flat convolution. This is a phase retrieval problem with nonnegativity. GS iterations:
- Start with initial a (positive).
- Frequency domain: set |A| = sqrt(FFT(C_target)) where C_target flat valid (after clipping D).
- Time domain: project to nonnegative and choose first n, normalize.
Repeat. This is exactly spectral_candidate with b=1 (flat target) but using fixed D_target not updated each time. We can implement both: fixed target GS, and adaptive beta.

Fixed target D: Compute C_target as flat T on 0..L-1, zeros tail. D=fft(C_target). Clip negatives, maybe add small epsilon to smooth. Then `|A| = sqrt(D) + eps`. For each iteration, phase = FFT(a) phase, Anew=sqrt(D)*phase, anew=ifft(Anew)[:n], clip/normalize. This is O(N log N) per iter; can run many. It directly enforces flat convolution in frequency magnitude (after clipping). Could find sequences with very flat valid c. But clipping D_target changes C_target; still.

Let's think if D_target nonnegative? FFT of rectangle length L: D_m = T * sum_{k=0}^{L-1} exp(2πi m k/N) (depending sign) = T * N/L * sinc(π m/L) with sign, many negative. Clipping makes a cap-like spectrum, resulting a with maybe square-rectangular? It might produce sequences.

Let's plan final algorithm staged:
1. Define helpers: `_next_pow2`, `_conv_full`, `_internal_obj`, `_softmax_p`, `_grad_from_p`, `_normalize`, `_positive`, `_evaluate_safe`, `_resize`, `_cosine_init`, `_random_init`, `_spectral_gs`, `_spectral_adaptive`, `_log_adam`, `_hard_refine`, maybe `_candidate_pool`.
2. `propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400)`:
   - `start_time`, `deadline = start + budget_s - 30` (leave margin).
   - Get parent array if global exists. If parent finite, make nonnegative (max(0)) and normalize. Also make a 1e-6 floor? Initial for log needs positive. 
   - Determine base lengths:
      * parent_len if 100<=len<=10000 else 3809.
      * other lengths: maybe `min(8191, parent_len*2)`, and a smaller `max(2048, parent_len//2)`. But to keep runtime, start with parent_len and one larger (8191) and one smaller (2048) if time.
   - For each length n in lengths sorted by priority [parent_len, 8191 if >parent, 2048 if <parent]:
       * Create a set of initial sequences:
         - If parent exists and len differs: resized parent, and maybe resized parent after log_bound? Also original if len matches.
         - Uniform.

Let's specify helper functions in detail.

`_next_pow2(m)`: return 1 << (max(1,m)-1).bit_length()? In Python:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`_normalize(a, target=1.0)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
s=float(a.sum())
if s <= 1e-300: return np.full_like(a, target/max(1,len(a)))
a *= target/s
# if any nonfinite? handle
return a
```

`_positive(a, floor_frac=1e-14)`:
```
a=_normalize(a)
f = floor_frac / len(a)
b = np.maximum(a, f)
return b / b.sum()
```

`_conv_full(a)`:
```
n=len(a)
if n < 256:
   c=np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
else:
   m=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(m)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:m]
c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
# remove tiny negative
c[c < 0] = 0.0 if small? If significant negative due to numerical? use np.maximum(c,0)
return c
```
Need `fa*fa` real? fa from rfft real array, product real; irfft returns real. Good.

`_obj(a)`:
```
if len(a)==0: inf
s=float(a.sum())
if s < 1e-12: inf
c=_conv_full(a)
mc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
# Since scale invariant, if s not 1, use 2n mc / s^2
return 2.0*len(a)*mc/(s*s)
```
Most normalized s=1.

`_softmax_p(c, tau)`:
```
if tau <= 0: p=zeros; idx=argmax; p[idx]=1; return p
z=c/tau; z-=z.max(); e=np.exp(z); se=e.sum(); if se<... uniform
p=e/se
```
But if tau very small and c max repeated, okay.

`_grad_from_p(p, a)`:
```
n=len(a)
if n < 512:
   return 2.0 * np.correlate(p, a, mode='valid')  # check valid returns length n
else:
   L=2*n-1
   p_full=np.zeros(L); p_full[:L]=p  # p already L
   a_rev=a[::-1]
   N=_next_pow2(L + n - 1 + 1) # 3n-2
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p_full,N)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,N)
   q=np.fft.irfft(fp*fa,N)
   g=2.0*q[n-1:2*n-1]
   return g
```
But `np.correlate(p, a, mode='valid')`: p length 2n-1, a length n, returns length n? We checked yes. Need confirm orientation: `np.correlate(p,a,'valid')` returns for each i sum p[i+j] a[j], as desired. Good.
`g` may have tiny negative; do `g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)`? Since p,a nonnegative, exact g nonnegative. FFT numerical could yield -1e-18. Clipping to 0 safe. But if error makes some positive? fine. However for gradient of smooth max, we want g. If we clip negative, sum maybe slightly changes; okay.
Normalize gradient? We'll do outside.

`_log_adam_run(a, deadline, tag, max_iterations, lr_init=0.2, tau_frac=0.25, use_spectral=True, spectral_every=200)`:

Pseudo:
```
def _log_adam_run(a, n, deadline, tag, rng, lr_init=0.2, tau_frac=0.2):
    a = _positive(a)
    c = _conv_full(a); mc=max; best_obj=2n mc; best_a=a.copy()
    w=np.log(a) # positive
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr=float(lr_init); b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    tau=max(mc*tau_frac, 1/(100*n))
    min_tau=max(mc*1e-3, 1/(1e6*n))
    stall=0
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       it+=1
       c=_conv_full(a); mc=max(c); o=2n*mc
       if o < best_obj - 1e-11: update best; stall=0
       else: stall+=1
       if stall>1000: maybe lr reset, random kick
       tau = max(tau*0.999, min_tau)
       p=_softmax_p(c,tau)
       g=_grad_from_p(p,a)
       gn=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if gn < 1e-300: break
       g = g/gn
       gw = a*(g - float(np.dot(a,g)))
       t+=1
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*gw
       v = b2*v + (1-b2)*gw*gw
       mh = m/(1-b1**t); vh = v/(1-b2**t)
       step = lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
       step = np.clip(step, -20,20)
       # backtrack
       accepted=False
       for f in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
           u = w + f*step
           u -= u.max()
           e=np.exp(u)
           s=e.sum()
           if s <=0 or not finite: break
           cand=e/s
           oc=_obj(cand)
           if oc < o - 1e-12:
               a=cand; w=u-(w.max?) We need set w to stable. We have u already max-shifted; assign w=u. But m,v correspond to old w constants? Shifting w by constant doesn't affect softmax but m/v are for w values; shifting all w by constant doesn't change gradient? Actually m/v are optimizer state in w; a constant shift changes w but not a; it could bias? Since adanomin states for shifted w are offset by same constant; if we shift w, we should ideally shift m/v? But since Adam update uses differences, adding constant to w doesn't matter if m/v not shifted? m/v are moments of gradient, independent of w value. Okay.
               lr = lr * (1.02 if f==1 else 1.0)
               accepted=True; break
           # if not accept, maybe try smaller
       if not accepted:
           lr = max(lr*0.7, 1e-5)
           if stall > 50:
               # small random log kick
               kick_sigma = 0.05 if stall<200 else 0.2
               u=w + rng.normal(0,kick_sigma,size=n); u-=u.max(); e=np.exp(u); a=e/e.sum(); 
               oc=_obj(a)
               if oc < best_obj: best update
               m = 0? maybe reset momentum partially
               m *= 0.5; v *= 0.5; stall=0
       # spectral candidate every e.g. 500 iterations or stall>300
       if use_spectral and (stall>200 or it % 2000 == 0):
           for beta in [0.5, 0.8]:
                cand=_spectral_adaptive(a,beta)
                oc=_obj(cand)
                if oc < best_obj - 1e-10:
                    a=_positive(cand); best update; w=log(a); m=0;v=0;lr=lr_init; stall=0; break
```
This may be slow due to `_obj(cand)` in backtrack computes conv (Nc). Each iteration up to 4 convs + one grad conv. For n=3809, 5*8192 FFT ~ maybe 5*0.3ms? Actually 8192 rfft ~ 0.02ms? Python overhead maybe 0.2ms; total ~1ms. 100k iters 100s. Good.

`_spectral_candidate` code:
```
def _spectral_candidate(a, beta=0.6):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
    ap=np.zeros(N); ap[:n]=a
    Af=np.fft.fft(ap)
    C=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ap,N)*np.fft.rfft(ap,N))  # faster conv
    C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    total=C.sum()
    if total<=0: return a
    C = C/total
    Cnew=C.copy()
    Ct=C[:L]
    T=1.0/L
    Ct_new=(1-beta)*Ct + beta*T
    # optional cap to target? pass cap None
    Cv = np.minimum(Ct_new, Ct_new) # no
    # sym
    Cv = 0.5*(Cv + Cv[::-1])
    Cnew[:L]=Cv
    Cnew[L:]=0.0
    D=np.fft.fft(Cnew)
    magD=np.abs(D)
    angD=np.angle(D)
    angA=np.angle(Af)
    rootAng=0.5*angD
    branch = (angA - rootAng)  # choose if close to rootAng+pi
    # cos(branch) < 0 => add pi
    branch_add = np.where(np.cos(branch) < 0.0, np.pi, 0.0)
    Anew = np.sqrt(magD) * np.exp(1j*(rootAng + branch_add))
    # enforce Hermitian symmetry maybe needed because branch choice may break:
    # For k=1..N-1, set Anew[N-k]=conj(Anew[k])
    # But doing for all: 
    idx=np.arange(1,N)
    Anew[N-idx] = np.conj(Anew[idx])
    # Hermitian on [1,N-1], Anew[0] real, Anew[N//2] real if even
    a_new=np.fft.ifft(Anew).real[:n]
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
    s=float(a_new.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return a
    a_new /= s
    return a_new
```
But setting Anew[N-idx]=conj(Anew[idx]) after branch may alter D. Maybe better choose branch for each pair consistently: For k and N-k, D[N-k]=conj(D[k]); rootAng[N-k]=0.5*angD[N-k] = 0.5*(-angD[k])? If ang principal, not exactly -? Should be -ang. Branch chosen independently may break. We can enforce by computing branch for k in first half and mirror. Simpler: For k=0..N//2, compute root; for k>N//2, ensure conj. But branch can differ? To get Hermitian, if A_k= r e^{iθ}, A_{N-k}=r e^{-iθ}. Need θ_{N-k} = -θ_k. D_{N-k}=r^2 e^{-i 2θ_k}. angle(D_{N-k}) = -2θ_k mod 2π. rootAng from angle gives -θ_k mod π? Since angle range [-π,π], 0.5 angle may wrap. Branch can adjust. To guarantee, we can compute full Anew by sqrt principal and then just take its real inverse? If principal sqrt preserves Hermitian? Check: principal sqrt of conj(z) = conj(principal sqrt(z)) except possible branch cut for negative real? It should for principal with branch cut negative real; if z on branch cut, conj same. Due numerical, okay. Could just use `Anew=np.sqrt(D)` (complex) and `a_new=ifft(Anew)`. The imaginary part should be ~0 if D Hermitian. This may be simpler and preserves symmetry (mostly). But branch discontinuities may cause poor time domain. The old-phase branch might improve but could break; we can mirror:
```
Anew=np.zeros(N,complex)
for k in range(N//2+1):
   # compute using old phase k
   Anew[k]=...

```

Active p function:
```
def _active_p(c, frac=1e-6):
   mc=float(np.max(c))
   if mc <=0: return None
   # include lags with c >= mc - max(1e-12, frac*mc)
   thr = max(0, mc - max(1e-12, frac*mc))
   p=np.zeros_like(c); p[c>=thr] = 1.0
   s=p.sum(); if s==0: p[argmax]=1; else p/=s
   return p
```

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`_safe_eval`:
```
def _safe_eval(seq):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in seq]
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    try:
        sc = evaluate_sequence(vals)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float('inf')
    return float(sc)
```
Need if `evaluate_sequence` undefined, NameError caught. Good.

`_conv_full` maybe cache? Not.
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    if n < 256:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        m = 2*n - 1
        N = _next_pow2(m)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    # If nonfinite, replace
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c
```
`fa*fa` for rfft complex okay. `irfft` of product is real. Need if `fa*fa` not exactly Hermitian due rfft length? Should be.

`_obj`:
```
def _obj(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return inf
   s=float(np.sum(a))
   if s < 1e-12: return inf
   c=_conv_full(a)
   mc=float(np.max(c))
   return 2.0*n*mc/(s*s)
```
If normalized s=1.

`_softmax_p`:
```
def _softmax_p(c, tau):
   L=len(c)
   if tau <= 1e-300:
       p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(c))] =1; return p
   z = c / tau
   z = z - float(np.max(z))
   e = np.exp(z)
   s = float(e.sum())
   if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       p=np.full(L,1.0/L); return p
   return e/s
```

`_active_p`:
```
def _active_p(c, frac=1e-5):
   mc=float(np.max(c))
   if mc <= 1e-300:
       p=np.ones_like(c)/len(c); return p
   thr = mc * max(0.0, 1.0 - max(frac, 1e-8))
   p = np.where(c >= thr, 1.0, 0.0)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <=0:
       p=np.zeros_like(c); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return p
   p /= s
   return p
```
But if `frac=1e-5`, thr=mc*(1-1e-5), includes very narrow. Good.

`_grad_from_p` as above. Use direct if n < 512. For n large:

L=2*n-1
N=_next_pow2(L + n - 1 + 1)  # 3n-1?
fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
q=np.fft.irfft(fp*fa,N)
g=2.0*q[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check end slice: length `2*n-1 - (n-1)=n`. Good.
`g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0); g = np.maximum(g,0.0)`.

```

best_a = a.copy(); best_o = _obj(a)
w = np.log(a)
m = np.zeros_like(a); v=...
t=0; lr=lr_init; stall=0; it=0

c0=_conv_full(a); mc0=max(c0); tau = Mc0*tau_frac
min_tau = Mc0*min_tau_frac
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   it += 1
   if it == 1: c = c0 else c = _conv_full(a)
   mc=float(np.max(c)); o=2*n*mc
   if o < best_o - 1e-11:
       best_o=o; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
   else:
       stall += 1
   # update tau:
   tau = max(tau*0.999, max(mc*min_tau_frac, 1e-18/n))
   if mode == 'hard' or tau <= mc*1e-5:
       p = _active_p(c, active_frac)
   else:
       p = _softmax_p(c, tau)
   g = _grad_from_p(p,a)
   gn = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0
   if gn < 1e-300:
       # no gradient, break or spectral
   g = g/gn
   gw = a * (g - float(np.dot(a,g)))
   t += 1
   b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*gw
   v = b2*v + (1-b2)*gw*gw
   bias1 = b1**t; bias2 = b2**t
   mh = m/(1-bias1)
   vh = v/(1-bias2)
   step = lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
   step = np.clip(step, -8.0, 8.0)
   # Backtracking line search
   accepted = False
   best_cand = None; best_cand_o = o
   for f in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
       u = w + f*step
       u = u - float(np.max(u))
       e = np.exp(u)
       s = float(e.sum())
       if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
           break
       cand = e/s
       oc = _obj(cand)
       if oc < best_cand_o - 1e-12:
           best_cand_o=oc; best_cand=cand; accepted=True
           # To save time, could break after first accepted? Use first f that improves current o? Maybe use best among tried.
   if accepted and best_cand is not None:
       a=best_cand; 
       # Need w corresponding to a: recompute w=log(a) to avoid drift? Since u candidate generated from w, but if f different, we didn't store u. recompute:
       w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
       w = w - float(np.max(w))
       # Reset momentum? No, keep.
       if f == 1.0: lr = min(lr*1.02, 1.0)
       else: lr = min(lr*1.0, lr+1e-5)
       o = best_cand_o
       if o < best_o - 1e-12: best update (already maybe)
   else:
       lr = max(lr*0.6, 1e-5)
       if stall > 30:
           # kick
           sigma = 0.03 + min(0.1, stall*1e-5)
           u = w + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
           u = u - float(np.max(u))
           e=np.exp(u); s=e.sum()
           if s>0:
              cand=e/s
              oc=_obj(cand)
              if oc < best_o - 1e-11:
                  a=cand; best update; m *=0; v *=0; stall=0; w=log...
              else:
                  # Keep current but reset momentum partially
                  m *=0.5; v *=0.5
           stall_counter? 
   # Spectral restart if stall large
   if use_spectral and (stall > 500 or (it % 3000 == 0 and stall > 100)):
       improved=False
       for beta in spectral_beta_choices:
           cand = _spectral_candidate(a, beta)
           oc = _obj(cand)
           if oc < best_o - 1e-10:
               a = _positive(cand); best update; w=log(a); m=np.zeros_like(a); v=np.zeros_like(a); t=0; lr=lr_init; tau=max(_max_conv(a)*tau_frac,...); stall=0; improved=True; break
       if improved:
           print(...)
   if it % 2000 == 0: print progress
```

`_spectral_candidate` implementation refined:
```
def _spectral_candidate(a, beta=0.6):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(max(4*n, L))  # 4n
    ap=np.zeros(N)
    ap[:n]=a
    # current C via rfft
    fa_r=np.fft.rfft(ap,N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(fa_r*fa_r,N)
    C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    s=C.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return a.copy()
    C = C/s
    Cnew=C.copy()
    T=1.0/L
    Cv=C[:L]
    blend = min(max(beta,0.0),1.5)
    Cv_new = (1.0 - blend)*Cv + blend*T
    # Optionally cap to current max * target? If blend >1 can raise lows > old peaks? no.
    Cv_new = np.maximum(Cv_new,0.0)
    Cv_new = 0.5*(Cv_new + Cv_new[::-1])
    Cnew[:L]=Cv_new
    Cnew[L:]=0.0
    # Ensure total sum exactly 1 (blend preserves, but sym maybe)
    tot=Cnew.sum()
    if tot <=0: return a.copy()
    Cnew /= tot
    D=np.fft.fft(Cnew)
    # Principal square root with Hermitian mirror
    A=np.sqrt(D)
    # Enforce Hermitian:
    if N > 1:
        half = N//2
        A[0] = np.real(A[0])
        # k=1..half-1 (and if N odd? N power of 2 even)
        if N % 2 == 0:
            A[half] = np.real(A[half])
            idx = np.arange(1, half)
            A[N-idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
        else:
            idx = np.arange(1, half+1)
            A[N-idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
    anew_full=np.fft.ifft(A).real
    a_new=anew_full[:n]
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
    ss=float(a_new.sum())
    if ss < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return a.copy()
    return a_new/ss
```
Will `np.sqrt(D)` branch be okay? D[0]=1 so A[0]=1. Good. If D has negative real on branch cut, sqrt pure imaginary; okay. The Hermitian mirror after sqrt may break D slightly but ensures real. Good.
Beta can be >1? We'll use <=1.

Could improve spectral by doing a few iterations alternating: candidate = _spectral_candidate(a,beta), then maybe accept if internal improves; if not, maybe try beta. The square root principal might not choose best branch relative to old A; we ignore old phase. Could use old phase branch to improve. Let's add optional branch using old `Af`? We already have `fa_r`; can convert to full `Af = np.fft.fft(ap)`? Cost. We can use `Af` for branch if desired. Square root principal enough. But phase branch can be chosen based on current A to maintain sequence. Let's implement with current Af and branch for first half? It is some code but could improve.
Using old phase branch in half vectorization:
```
Af = np.fft.fft(ap)  # full
D = np.fft.fft(Cnew)
half=N//2+1
Dh=D[:half]; Ah=Af[:half]
magDh=np.abs(Dh); angDh=np.angle(Dh); angAh=np.angle(Ah)
root=0.5*angDh
branch=np.where(np.cos(angAh - root) < 0.0, np.pi, 0.0)
AnewH = np.sqrt(magDh)*np.exp(1j*(root+branch))
# place half and mirror
Anew=np.zeros(N, complex)
Anew[:half]=AnewH
if N%2==0:
   # indices 1..N/2-1 mirror
   if half-1 > 1:
      Anew[N-half+1:] = np.conj(AnewH[1:half-1][::-1])
   Anew[N//2]=np.real(AnewH[N//2])
else:
   Anew[N-half+1:] = np.conj(AnewH[1:half][::-1])

Need top-level `_resample(a, n)`:
```
def _resample(a, n):
   m=len(a)
   if m==n: return a.copy()
   if m==1: return np.full(n, a[0])
   x_old=np.arange(m, dtype=float); x_new=np.linspace(0.0, m-1.0, n)
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   return _normalize(b)
```

def _get_parent():
   try:
       p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
   except Exception:
       return None
   if p.ndim !=1 or p.size ==0: return None
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return None
   p = np.maximum(p,0.0)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <=0: return None
   return p/s
```

Now, initial spectral GS: We'll call with `iters=40`, `betas=(0.35,0.55,0.8)`. Function:
```
def _spectral_improve(a, iters=20, betas=(0.4,0.7), deadline=None, tag=''):
   a=_positive(a); best=a; best_o=_obj(a); no_improve=0
   for i in range(iters):
      if deadline and time>deadline: break
      improved=False
      for beta in betas:
          cand=_spectral_candidate(a,beta)
          o=_obj(cand)
          if o < best_o - 1e-10:
             a=cand; best_o=o; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve >= 3: break
      else: no_improve=0
   return best,best_o
```
This only accepts improvements, so if a beta slightly worsens now but part of sequence improves, not. Could use currently `a` even if not improve? Maybe. But for seeding, accept improved only safe. We can also try a few from uniform and keep best even if final obj worse than init? Not needed.

        if o < top[max_i][1]: top[max_i]=(a,o)
```

Let's write `_log_descent_run` carefully to avoid bugs in time/state.

Pseudo full:
```
def _log_descent_run(a0, deadline, tag, rng, mode='mixed', lr_init=0.12, tau_frac=0.25, min_tau_frac=1e-4, active_frac=1e-5, use_spectral=True):
    a = _positive(a0)
    n=len(a)
    c = _conv_full(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy(); best_o = 2.0*n*mc
    w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)); w -= float(np.max(w))
    m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr=float(lr_init); b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    tau = max(mc*tau_frac, 1.0/(100.0*n))
    stall=0; it=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    Lags = 2*n-1
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it += 1
       # compute c (could reuse previous? we need after update)
       c = _conv_full(a) if it>1 else c
       mc = float(np.max(c)); o=2*n*mc
       if o < best_o - 1e-11:
           best_o=o; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
       else:
           stall += 1
       # tau update
       min_tau = max(mc*min_tau_frac, 1e-18/n)
       tau = max(tau*0.999, min_tau)
       use_hard = (mode == 'hard') or (tau <= mc*1e-5)
       if use_hard:
           weights = _active_p(c, active_frac)
           allow_neg = False
       else:
           p = _softmax_p(c, tau)
           # equalization schedule: use signed equality every other iteration? Use blend factor depending on it
           avg = float(c.sum())/Lags
           w_eq = c - avg
           nw = float(np.max(np.abs(w_eq)))
           if nw < 1e-300:
               w_eq = np.zeros_like(c)
           else:
               w_eq = w_eq/nw
           # early: more equality; later: more peak
           if mode == 'eq':
               weights = w_eq
           elif it < 5000:
               weights = 0.35*p + 0.65*w_eq
           else:
               weights = 0.7*p + 0.3*w_eq
           allow_neg = True
       wn = float(np.max(np.abs(weights)))
       if wn <= 1e-300: 
           # no direction, try spectral or break
       else:
           weights = weights / wn
           g = _grad_from_p(weights, a, allow_negative=allow_neg)
           gn = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
           if gn < 1e-300:
               g = None
       if g is not None:
           gw = a * (g - float(np.dot(a, g)))
           t += 1
           m = b1*m + (1-b1)*gw
           v = b2*v + (1-b2)*gw*gw
           bias1 = b1**t
           bias2 = b2**t
           mh = m/(1-bias1)
           vh = v/(1-bias2)
           step = lr * mh/(np.sqrt(vh) + eps)
           step = np.clip(step, -10.0, 10.0)
           accepted=False
           best_cand=None; best_f=0.0; best_cand_o=o
           for f in (1.0, 0.35, 0.1):
               u = w + f*step
               u = u - float(np.max(u))
               e = np.exp(u)
               s = float(e.sum())
               if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): break
               cand = e/s
               oc = _obj(cand)
               if oc < best_cand_o - 1e-12:
                   best_cand_o = oc; best_cand=cand; best_f=f; accepted=True
           if accepted:
               a=best_cand
               w = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); w -= float(np.max(w))
               if best_f >= 0.99: lr = min(lr*1.03, 0.8)
               else: lr = max(lr*0.9, 1e-5) # maybe not shrink if partial? we can keep
               o=best_cand_o
               if o < best_o - 1e-12:
                  best_o=o; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
           else:
               lr = max(lr*0.65, 1e-5)
               if stall > 20:
                   sigma = 0.02 + min(0.15, 1e-4*stall)
                   u = w + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
                   u = u - float(np.max(u))
                   e=np.exp(u); s=e.sum()
                   if s>1e-300:
                       cand=e/s; oc=_obj(cand)
                       if oc < best_o - 1e-11:
                           a=cand; best update; m*=0; v*=0; t=0; w=log; stall=0
                       else:
                           m *= 0.5; v *=0.5
       # spectral restart
       if use_spectral and stall > 250:
           improved=False
           for beta in (0.5,0.8,1.0):
               cand = _spectral_candidate(a, beta)
               oc = _obj(cand)
               if oc < best_o - 1e-10:
                   a=_positive(cand); best update; w=log; m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0; lr=lr_init; c=_conv_full(a); mc=max(c); tau=max(mc*tau_frac,...); stall=0; improved=True; break
           if improved:
               print(...)
       if it % 2000 == 0 or time>last_print+30:
           print(f"[{tag}] it={it} obj={best_o:.6f} cur={o:.6f} lr={lr:.4f}")
           last_print=time.monotonic()
    return best_a, best_o
```

```
A = np.sqrt(D)
if N > 2:
    half = N // 2
    A[0] = np.real(A[0])
    if N % 2 == 0:
        A[half] = np.real(A[half])
        if half > 1:
            idx = np.arange(1, half)
            A[N - idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
    else:
        idx = np.arange(1, half+1)
        A[N - idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
```

`_compute_top(lst, k)`:
```
def _top_k(lst, k):
   best=[]
   for item in lst:
      if item[1] >= float('inf'): continue
      if len(best)<k:
         best.append(item)
      else:
         max_i=0
         for j in range(1,k):
            if best[j][1] > best[max_i][1]: max_i=j
         if item[1] < best[max_i][1]: best[max_i]=item
         else: continue
   return best
```
Works.

def _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, a):
   if len(checkpoints) < 20 and a is not None:
       checkpoints.append(a.copy())
```

Check syntax for top-level functions:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        n = max(1, len(a))
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (target / s)
    return a
```

`_positive`:
```
def _positive(a, floor_frac=1e-14):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = len(a)
    f = floor_frac / max(1, n)
    b = np.maximum(a, f)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    return b / s
```

`_sparsify`:
```
def _sparsify(a, threshold=1e-12):
    b=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    b[b<threshold]=0.0
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return _normalize(a)
    return b/s
```

`_safe_eval` as above.

`_conv_full`:
Need if `fa*fa` in rfft for N? `np.fft.rfft` returns complex128. `irfft` expects complex length N//2+1. Good.
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
       return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 256:
       c = np.convolve(a, a, mode='full')
    else:
       m = 2*n - 1
       N = _next_pow2(m)
       fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
       c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```

`_obj` as above.

`_softmax_p`: if tau very small but c positive, p one-hot. Use `np.argmax`. 

`_active_p`: choose top lags whose c >= `mc*(1-frac)`. If `frac=1e-5` too narrow; maybe include top 10 always:
```
def _active_p(c, frac=1e-4):
   mc=float(np.max(c)); L=len(c)
   p=np.zeros(L)
   # top 8
   if L>0:
      topk=int(min(8, L))
      if topk == L:
         idx=np.arange(L)
      else:
         idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
      p[idx]=1.0
   # active threshold
   if mc > 0:
      thr = mc * max(0.0, 1.0 - max(frac, 1e-6))
      p = np.where(c >= thr, 1.0, p)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: p[np.argmax(c)]=1; return p
   p/=s
   return p
```
This ensures active set not empty. `np.argpartition` on length 7617 okay.

`_grad_from_p` as above.
```
def _grad_from_p(w, a, allow_negative=False):
   n=len(a)
   L=2*n-1
   if w.size != L:
       w = np.resize(w, L)
   if n < 512:
       g = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, a, mode='valid')
   else:
       N = _next_pow2(L + n - 1 + 1)
       fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
       fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
       q = np.fft.irfft(fw * fa, N)
       g = 2.0 * q[n-1:2*n-1]
   g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
   g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
   if not allow_negative:
       g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
   return g
```
Check N: L + n - 1 = (2n-1)+n-1=3n-2; +1 safe 3n-1. `_next_pow2(L + n - 1 + 1)`. Good.

Direct `np.correlate(w, a, 'valid')` length? For n=1, length1. Good.

def _periodic_init(n, pattern):
    pat=np.asarray(pattern,float)
    m=len(pat)
    rep=(n+m-1)//m
    b=np.empty(n)
    for i in range(n):
       b[i]=pat[i % m]
    return _normalize(b)
```

`_spectral_candidate` as above. Need if `N > 1` and N even.

```
def _spectral_candidate(a, beta=0.6):
   n=len(a)
   if n <=1: return a.copy()
   L=2*n-1
   N=_next_pow2(max(4*n, L))
   ap=np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
   ap[:n]=a
   fa_r=np.fft.rfft(ap, N)
   C=np.fft.irfft(fa_r*fa_r, N)
   C=np.where(np.isfinite(C), C, 0.0)
   C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
   tot=float(C.sum())
   if tot <= 1e-300:
      return a.copy()
   C=C/tot
   Cnew=C.copy()
   T=1.0/L
   Cv=C[:L].copy()
   b=float(min(max(beta,0.0),1.0))
   Cv=(1.0-b)*Cv + b*T
   Cv=np.maximum(Cv,0.0)
   Cv=0.5*(Cv + Cv[::-1])
   Cnew[:L]=Cv
   Cnew[L:]=0.0
   tot2=float(Cnew.sum())
   if tot2 <= 1e-300: return a.copy()
   Cnew=Cnew/tot2
   D=np.fft.fft(Cnew)
   A=np.sqrt(D)
   if N > 1:
      half=N//2
      A[0]=np.real(A[0])
      if N % 2 == 0:
         A[half]=np.real(A[half])
         if half > 1:
            idx=np.arange(1, half)
            A[N-idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
      else:
         idx=np.arange(1, half+1)
         A[N-idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
   anew=np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
   anew=np.where(np.isfinite(anew), anew, 0.0)
   anew=np.maximum(anew,0.0)
   s=float(anew.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300: return a.copy()
   return anew/s
```
Potential: `Cv[::-1]` view; `0.5*(Cv + Cv[::-1])` okay.

`_spectral_improve` top-level:
```
def _spectral_improve(a, iters=30, betas=(0.4,0.7,1.0), tag=''):
   a=_positive(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_o=_obj(a)
   no=0
   for i in range(iters):
      improved=False
      for beta in betas:
         cand=_spectral_candidate(a,beta)
         o=_obj(cand)
         if o < best_o - 1e-10:
             a=_positive(cand); best=a.copy(); best_o=o; improved=True
             break
      if not improved:
         no +=1
         if no>=3: break
      else: no=0
   return best,best_o
```
If spectral from uniform decreases slowly, it may accept small improvements. Good.

`_log_descent_run` code need be syntactically correct. Let's write it fully with attention.

```
def _log_descent_run(a0, deadline, tag, rng, mode="mixed", lr_init=0.15, tau_frac=0.25, min_tau_frac=1e-4, active_frac=1e-5, use_spectral=True):
    a = _positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2*n - 1
    c = _conv_full(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_o = 2.0*n*mc
    w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    w = w - float(np.max(w))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = float(lr_init)
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    tau = max(mc*tau_frac, 1.0/(100.0*n))
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if it > 1:
            c = _conv_full(a)
        mc = float(np.max(c))
        o = 2.0*n*mc
        if o < best_o - 1e-11:
            best_o = o
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
        min_tau = max(mc*min_tau_frac, 1e-18/n)
        tau = max(tau*0.999, min_tau)
        g = None
        if use_hard? no variable but mode.
        use_hard = (mode == "hard") or (tau <= mc*1e-5)
        if use_hard:
            weights = _active_p(c, active_frac)
            allow_neg = False
        else:
            p = _softmax_p(c, tau)
            avg = float(np.sum(c))
            if avg <= 0: avg = 1.0
            avg = avg / max(1, L)
            w_eq = c - avg
            nw = float(np.max(np.abs(w_eq)))
            if nw < 1e-300:
                w_eq = np.zeros_like(c)
            else:
                w_eq = w_eq / nw
            if mode == "eq":
                weights = w_eq
                allow_neg = True
            elif it < 8000:
                weights = 0.30*p + 0.70*w_eq
                allow_neg = True
            else:
                weights = 0.65*p + 0.35*w_eq
                allow_neg = True
        wn = float(np.max(np.abs(weights)))
        if wn > 1e-300:
            weights = weights / wn
            g = _grad_from_p(weights, a, allow_negative=allow_neg)
            gn = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if gn < 1e-300:
                g = None
        if g is not None:
            gw = a * (g - float(np.dot(a, g)))
            t += 1
            m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*gw
            v = b2*v + (1.0-b2)*gw*gw
            bias1 = b1**t
            bias2 = b2**t
            mh = m / (1.0 - bias1)
            vh = v / (1.0 - bias2)
            step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)
            step = np.clip(step, -10.0, 10.0)
            accepted = False
            best_cand = None
            best_cand_o = o
            best_f = 0.0
            for f in (1.0, 0.35, 0.1):
                u = w + f*step
                u = u - float(np.max(u))
                e = np.exp(u)
                s = float(e.sum())
                if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    break
                cand = e / s
                oc = _obj(cand)
                if oc < best_cand_o - 1e-12:
                    best_cand_o = oc
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_f = f
                    accepted = True
            if accepted and best_cand is not None:
                a = best_cand
                w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                w = w - float(np.max(w))
                if best_f >= 0.99:
                    lr = min(lr*1.03, 0.8)
                else:
                    lr = max(lr*0.92, 1e-5)
                o = best_cand_o
                if o < best_o - 1e-12:
                    best_o = o
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    stall = 0
            else:
                lr = max(lr*0.65, 1e-5)
                if stall > 20:
                    sigma = 0.02 + min(0.15, 1e-4 * stall)
                    u = w + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
                    u = u - float(np.max(u))
                    e = np.exp(u)
                    s = float(e.sum())
                    if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
                       cand = e/s
                       oc = _obj(cand)
                       if oc < best_o - 1e-11:
                           a=cand; best_o=oc; best_a=a.copy(); m *=0; v*=0; t=0; w=log; stall=0
                       else:
                           m *= 0.5; v *= 0.5
        if use_spectral and stall > 250:
            improved = False
            for beta in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
                cand = _spectral_candidate(a, beta)
                oc = _obj(cand)
                if oc < best_o - 1e-8:
                    a = _positive(cand)
                    c = _conv_full(a)
                    mc = float(np.max(c))
                    best_o = oc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    w = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); w-=w.max()
                    m = np.zeros_like(a); v = np.zeros_like(a); t=0; lr=float(lr_init)
                    tau = max(mc*tau_frac, 1.0/(100.0*n))
                    stall = 0
                    improved = True
                    print(f"[{tag}] spectral beta={beta} obj={best_o:.6f}")
                    break
        if it % 2000 == 0 or time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
            print(f"[{tag}] it={it} best={best_o:.6f} cur={o:.6f} stall={stall} lr={lr:.4f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()
    return best_a.copy(), float(best_o)
```

Now `_top_k` and `_add_checkpoint`:
```
def _top_k(items, k):
    best=[]
    for item in items:
       if item is None: continue
       a,o=item
       if not np.isfinite(o): continue
       if len(best) < k:
          best.append(item)
       else:
          mi=0
          for j in range(1,k):
             if best[j][1] > best[mi][1]: mi=j
          if o < best[mi][1]:
             best[mi]=item
    return best
```

def _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, a):
   if checkpoints is None or a is None: return
   if len(checkpoints) < 24:
       checkpoints.append((a.copy(), _obj(a)))
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/sum(a)^2 for nonnegative a.

The method uses:
- FFT-based linear self-convolution and gradient evaluation.
- Log-softmax parameterization a_i > 0, sum a_i = 1, with Adam updates.
- A mix of entropy-smoothed maximum-convolution gradients, active-set
  subgradient terms, and a "flattening" term that pushes low lags upward.
- Spectral restarts that project toward a flatter linear autocorrelation.
- A few different sequence lengths and local refinements.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        n = max(1, len(a))
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (target / s)
    return a


def _positive(a, floor_frac=1e-14):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = len(a)
    f = floor_frac / max(1, n)
    b = np.maximum(a, f)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def _sparsify(a, threshold=1e-12):
    b = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    b[b < threshold] = 0.0
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return _normalize(a)
    return b / s


def _safe_eval(seq):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in seq]
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        sc = evaluate_sequence(vals)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf")
    return float(sc)


def _conv_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 256:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        m = 2 * n - 1
        N = _next_pow2(m)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _obj(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_full(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def _softmax_p(c, tau):
    L = len(c)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if tau <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    z = c / tau
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _active_p(c, frac=1e-4):
    L = len(c)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    topk = int(min(8, L))
    if topk > 0:
        if topk == L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
        p[idx] = 1.0
    if mc > 0.0:
        thr = mc * max(0.0, 1.0 - max(frac, 1e-6))
        p = np.where(c >= thr, 1.0, p)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return p / s


def _grad_from_p(w, a, allow_negative=False):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != L:
        w = np.resize(w, L)
    if n < 512:
        g = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, a, mode="valid")
    else:
        N = _next_pow2(L + n - 1 + 1)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
        q = np.fft.irfft(fw * fa, N)
        g = 2.0 * q[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    if not allow_negative:
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def _uniform_init(n):
    return np.full(max(1, int(n)), 1.0 / max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)


def _cosine_init(n, amp, freq, phase=0.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * i / n + phase)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return _normalize(a)


def _random_init(n, rng, sigma=0.5):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = 1.0 + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return _normalize(a)


def _periodic_init(n, pattern):
    pat = np.asarray(pattern, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(pat)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    reps = (n + m - 1) // m
    b = np.tile(pat, reps)[:n]
    return _normalize(b)


def _get_parent():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return None
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return p / s


def _resample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = len(a)
    if m == 0:
        return _uniform_init(n)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, a[0], dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize(b)


def _spectral_candidate(a, beta=0.6):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy()
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(max(4 * n, L))
    ap = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    ap[:n] = a

    fa_r = np.fft.rfft(ap, N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(fa_r * fa_r, N)
    C = np.where(np.isfinite(C), C, 0.0)
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    tot = float(C.sum())
    if tot <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    C = C / tot

    Cnew = C.copy()
    T = 1.0 / L
    Cv = C[:L].copy()
    b = float(min(max(beta, 0.0), 1.0))
    Cv = (1.0 - b) * Cv + b * T
    Cv = np.maximum(Cv, 0.0)
    Cv = 0.5 * (Cv + Cv[::-1])
    Cnew[:L] = Cv
    Cnew[L:] = 0.0

    tot2 = float(Cnew.sum())
    if tot2 <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    Cnew = Cnew / tot2

    D = np.fft.fft(Cnew)
    A = np.sqrt(D)

    if N > 1:
        half = N // 2
        A[0] = np.real(A[0])
        if N % 2 == 0:
            A[half] = np.real(A[half])
            if half > 1:
                idx = np.arange(1, half)
                A[N - idx] = np.conj(A[idx])
        else:
            idx = np.arange(1, half + 1)
            A[N - idx] = np.conj(A[idx])

    anew_full = np.fft.ifft(A).real[:n]
    anew_full = np.where(np.isfinite(anew_full), anew_full, 0.0)
    anew_full = np.maximum(anew_full, 0.0)
    s = float(anew_full.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return anew_full / s


def _spectral_improve(a, iters=30, betas=(0.4, 0.7, 1.0), tag=""):
    a = _positive(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_o = _obj(a)
    no_improve = 0

    for i in range(iters):
        improved = False
        for beta in betas:
            cand = _spectral_candidate(a, beta)
            o = _obj(cand)
            if o < best_o - 1e-10:
                a = _positive(cand)
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_o = o
                improved = True
                break
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break
    return best_a, float(best_o)


def _top_k(items, k):
    best = []
    k = max(1, int(k))
    for item in items:
        if item is None:
            continue
        a, o = item
        if not np.isfinite(o):
            continue
        if len(best) < k:
            best.append(item)
        else:
            mi = 0
            for j in range(1, k):
                if best[j][1] > best[mi][1]:
                    mi = j
            if o < best[mi][1]:
                best[mi] = item
    return best


def _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, a, o):
    if checkpoints is None:
        return
    if a is None:
        return
    if len(checkpoints) < 24:
        checkpoints.append((a.copy(), float(o)))


def _initial_candidates(n, rng, parent_res):
    cands = []

    if parent_res is not None:
        cands.append(_positive(parent_res))

    cands.append(_uniform_init(n))
    cands.append(_cosine_init(n, 0.30, 1, 0.0))
    cands.append(_cosine_init(n, 0.55, 2, 0.7))
    cands.append(_cosine_init(n, 0.45, 5, 2.1))
    cands.append(_cosine_init(n, 0.80, 1, 1.5))

    cands.append(_random_init(n, rng, 0.4))
    cands.append(_random_init(n, rng, 1.0))

    cands.append(_periodic_init(n, [1.0, 1.0, 0.0]))
    cands.append(_periodic_init(n, [1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0]))
    cands.append(_periodic_init(n, [1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0]))

    res = []
    for a in cands:
        b = _positive(a)
        o = _obj(b)
        if np.isfinite(o):
            res.append((b, float(o)))
    return res


def _log_descent_run(a0, deadline, tag, rng, mode="mixed", lr_init=0.15,
                     tau_frac=0.25, min_tau_frac=1e-4, active_frac=1e-5,
                     use_spectral=True):
    a = _positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    c = _conv_full(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_o = float(2.0 * n * mc)

    w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    w = w - float(np.max(w))

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0

    lr = float(lr_init)
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    tau = max(mc * tau_frac, 1.0 / (100.0 * n))
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if it > 1:
            c = _conv_full(a)

        mc = float(np.max(c))
        o = float(2.0 * n * mc)

        if o < best_o - 1e-11:
            best_o = o
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        min_tau = max(mc * min_tau_frac, 1e-18 / n)
        tau = max(tau * 0.999, min_tau)

        g = None

        use_hard = (mode == "hard") or (tau <= mc * 1e-5)

        if use_hard:
            weights = _active_p(c, active_frac)
            allow_neg = False
        else:
            p = _softmax_p(c, tau)
            s_c = float(np.sum(c))
            if s_c <= 0.0:
                s_c = 1.0
            avg = s_c / max(1, L)

            w_eq = c - avg
            nw = float(np.max(np.abs(w_eq)))
            if nw < 1e-300:
                w_eq = np.zeros_like(c)
            else:
                w_eq = w_eq / nw

            if mode == "eq":
                weights = w_eq
                allow_neg = True
            elif it < 8000:
                weights = 0.30 * p + 0.70 * w_eq
                allow_neg = True
            else:
                weights = 0.65 * p + 0.35 * w_eq
                allow_neg = True

        wn = float(np.max(np.abs(weights)))
        if wn > 1e-300:
            weights = weights / wn
            g = _grad_from_p(weights, a, allow_negative=allow_neg)
            gn = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
            if gn < 1e-300:
                g = None

        if g is not None:
            gw = a * (g - float(np.dot(a, g)))

            t += 1
            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gw
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * gw * gw

            bias1 = b1 ** t
            bias2 = b2 ** t
            mh = m / (1.0 - bias1)
            vh = v / (1.0 - bias2)
            step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)
            step = np.clip(step, -10.0, 10.0)

            accepted = False
            best_cand = None
            best_cand_o = o
            best_f = 0.0

            for f in (1.0, 0.35, 0.1):
                u = w + f * step
                u = u - float(np.max(u))
                e = np.exp(u)
                s = float(e.sum())
                if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    break
                cand = e / s
                oc = _obj(cand)
                if oc < best_cand_o - 1e-12:
                    best_cand_o = oc
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_f = f
                    accepted = True

            if accepted and best_cand is not None:
                a = best_cand
                w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                w = w - float(np.max(w))

                if best_f >= 0.99:
                    lr = min(lr * 1.03, 0.8)
                else:
                    lr = max(lr * 0.92, 1e-5)

                o = best_cand_o
                if o < best_o - 1e-12:
                    best_o = o
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    stall = 0
            else:
                lr = max(lr * 0.65, 1e-5)

                if stall > 20:
                    sigma = 0.02 + min(0.15, 1e-4 * stall)
                    u = w + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
                    u = u - float(np.max(u))
                    e = np.exp(u)
                    s = float(e.sum())
                    if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
                        cand = e / s
                        oc = _obj(cand)
                        if oc < best_o - 1e-11:
                            a = cand
                            best_a = cand.copy()
                            best_o = oc
                            m = np.zeros_like(a)
                            v = np.zeros_like(a)
                            t = 0
                            w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                            w = w - float(np.max(w))
                            o = oc
                            stall = 0
                        else:
                            m *= 0.5
                            v *= 0.5

        if use_spectral and stall > 250:
            improved = False
            for beta in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
                cand = _spectral_candidate(a, beta)
                oc = _obj(cand)
                if oc < best_o - 1e-10:
                    a = _positive(cand)
                    c = _conv_full(a)
                    mc = float(np.max(c))
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_o = oc
                    w = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                    w = w - float(np.max(w))
                    m = np.zeros_like(a)
                    v = np.zeros_like(a)
                    t = 0
                    lr = float(lr_init)
                    tau = max(mc * tau_frac, 1.0 / (100.0 * n))
                    stall = 0
                    improved = True
                    print(f"[{tag}] spectral beta={beta} obj={best_o:.6f}")
                    break

        if it % 2000 == 0 or time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
            print(f"[{tag}] it={it} best={best_o:.6f} cur={o:.6f} stall={stall} lr={lr:.4f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best_a.copy(), float(best_o)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    checkpoints = []

    parent = _get_parent()

    primary_len = None
    if parent is not None and 50 <= len(parent) <= 20000:
        primary_len = int(len(parent))
    if primary_len is None:
        primary_len = 3809

    lengths = [primary_len]
    if primary_len < 8000:
        lengths.append(8191)
    else:
        lengths.append(5000)
    if int(budget_s) > 1600:
        lengths.append(12288)

    clean_lengths = []
    for L in lengths:
        L = int(max(64, L))
        if L not in clean_lengths:
            clean_lengths.append(L)
        if len(clean_lengths) >= 3:
            break
    lengths = clean_lengths

    all_items = []

    for L in lengths:
        parent_res = _resample(parent, L) if parent is not None else None
        cands = _initial_candidates(L, rng, parent_res)

        top_cands = _top_k(cands, 2)
        for a_seed, o_seed in top_cands:
            b, bo = _spectral_improve(
                a_seed,
                iters=25,
                betas=(0.4, 0.7, 1.0),
                tag=f"spectral-seed-{L}"
            )
            cands.append((b, bo))

        topL = _top_k(cands, 5)
        all_items.extend(topL)

    init_top = _top_k(all_items, 5)

    if not init_top:
        a = _uniform_init(primary_len)
        o = _obj(a)
        init_top = [(a, o)]

    global_a, global_o = init_top[0]
    _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, global_a, global_o)
    for a, o in init_top:
        _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, a, o)

    print(f"init best n={len(global_a)} internal_obj={global_o:.6f} eval={_safe_eval(global_a):.6f}")

    window = max(240.0, float(budget_s) - 220.0)
    slots = [
        int(0.30 * window),
        int(0.16 * window),
        int(0.12 * window),
        int(0.10 * window),
    ]
    modes = ["mixed", "eq", "mixed", "hard"]
    lrs = [0.15, 0.12, 0.12, 0.05]

    for idx, (a_init, o_init) in enumerate(init_top[:4]):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break

        slot = slots[idx] if idx < len(slots) else int(0.05 * window)
        slot = max(30, slot)
        end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + slot)

        if idx == 0 or o_init <= global_o + 0.05:
            start_a = a_init
        else:
            start_a = global_a

        mode = modes[idx] if idx < len(modes) else "hard"
        lr = lrs[idx] if idx < len(lrs) else (0.05 if mode == "hard" else 0.10)

        a_out, o_out = _log_descent_run(
            start_a,
            end,
            f"run{idx}",
            rng,
            mode=mode,
            lr_init=lr,
            use_spectral=(mode != "hard")
        )

        if o_out < global_o - 1e-12:
            global_a = a_out
            global_o = o_out
            _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, global_a, global_o)
            print(f"[run{idx}] improved to {global_o:.6f} n={len(global_a)}")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        a_out, o_out = _log_descent_run(
            global_a,
            deadline - 5.0,
            "final-hard",
            rng,
            mode="hard",
            lr_init=0.05,
            use_spectral=False
        )
        if o_out < global_o - 1e-12:
            global_a = a_out
            global_o = o_out
            _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, global_a, global_o)
            print(f"[final-hard] improved to {global_o:.6f} n={len(global_a)}")

    sp_a = _sparsify(global_a, 1e-12)
    sp_o = _obj(sp_a)
    if sp_o < global_o - 1e-10:
        global_a = sp_a
        global_o = sp_o

    _add_checkpoint(checkpoints, global_a, global_o)

    final_items = list(checkpoints)
    final_items.append((global_a, global_o))

    for a, _o in _top_k(final_items, 3):
        sp = _sparsify(a, 1e-11)
        final_items.append((sp, _obj(sp)))

    best_return = global_a
    best_score = float("inf")

    for a, _o in _top_k(final_items, 12):
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = _normalize(a)
        sc = _safe_eval(a)
        internal = _obj(a)
        score = sc if np.isfinite(sc) else internal
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_return = a
            print(f"final candidate n={len(a)} internal={internal:.6f} actual={sc:.6f}")

    if best_return is None:
        best_return = _uniform_init(primary_len)

    result = []
    for x in best_return:
        try:
            y = float(x)
        except Exception:
            y = 1.0
        if not np.isfinite(y):
            y = 1.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        if y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        result.append(y)

    if len(result) == 0 or sum(result) < 0.01:
        result = [1.0]

    return result
```
